<h3><a href="/polls/5940">The Scariest Halloween</a></h3>
<a href="/polls/5940"><img alt="Halloween decoration poll" class="mt15 article-activity-image text-center" data-original="https://uploads.kidzworld.com/poll/original/5940/halloween-decoration-poll.jpg" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODdhAQABAPAAAMPDwwAAACwAAAAAAQABAAACAkQBADs="/></a>
<div class="article_rt">
<a class="show-liker rateLove" href="/login"><i aria-hidden="true" class="fa fa-thumbs-up thumb_color"></i> <span class="sky_color rateNum" id="rate_5940">26</span></a>
</div>
<form accept-charset="UTF-8" action="/polls/5940/vote" data-remote="true" id="skyscraper-poll-form" method="post"><input name="utf8" type="hidden" value="✓"/><input name="authenticity_token" type="hidden" value="hl7AvhxN1+lruuDV4H8DXpe3jGi/8NYOKn+r2Ioe7Kk61XHvkvd2Dct8V+Ljqi6pNfV8y/er1iYzDpzAtkrLKA=="/>
<div class="article_right_bottom_text">
<p>What's the scariest Halloween decoration ever?</p>
<div class="radio_main_text" id="poll-form">
<ul>
<li>
<input id="0-option" name="poll[answer]" type="radio" value="1"/>
<label for="0-option">A pair of gnarled hands digging up through someone's yard.</label>
<div class="check"></div>
</li>
<li>
<input id="1-option" name="poll[answer]" type="radio" value="2"/>
<label for="1-option">A tall hooded and silent figure lurking behind bushes</label>
<div class="check"></div>
</li>
<li>
<input id="2-option" name="poll[answer]" type="radio" value="3"/>
<label for="2-option">A mechanical but realistic spider the size of a dinner plate</label>
<div class="check"></div>
</li>
<li>
<input id="3-option" name="poll[answer]" type="radio" value="4"/>
<label for="3-option">I can't answer, they are all too freaky!</label>
<div class="check"></div>
</li>
</ul>
